test-time reinforcement learning 在小答案空间上的失效机制。已有 TTRL 做法通常对每个无标注测试题采样多条回答，用多数票答案当 pseudo-label，再更新模型去偏向这个答案。这个方法在数学自由生成任务上能工作，但在医疗多选题上会出现一个很典型的塌缩：模型输出越来越一致，diversity 快速下降，但准确率并没有同步提升。作者要解决的核心问题不是“医疗题更难”，而是更具体地问：当答案空间很小、结构很强时，基于最终答案一致性的自训练奖励为什么会系统性失真，以及能否用过程级奖励替代答案级奖励来修复这种失真。","importance":"从严格的 pretrain 视角看，这不是预训练论文，而是 post-train / test-time adaptation / RL signal design 的工作。但它对基座研究仍有直接启发，因为它揭示了一个很通用的训练信号问题：当监督来自模型自身输出时，奖励结构和答案空间几何会决定自举是否稳定。这个结论不只适用于医疗 QA。任何依赖 self-consistency、majority vote、pseudo-label self-training、甚至部分 verifier-lite 方案的后训练流程，都可能遇到类似偏差。论文的重要性在于，它没有停留在“某领域上效果不好”的经验观察，而是通过控制变量实验把失败原因定位到 answer-space structure，并进一步提出了 reward reasoning, not answer agreement 的修复路径。贡献量级上，它更像一篇对 test-time RL 信号设计有明确诊断价值的论文，而不是通用医学应用工作。","technical_approach":"论文先做机制诊断，再给方法。诊断部分的关键概念是 Lucky Hit 机制在不同答案空间下的行为差异。已有 TTRL 依赖这样一种隐含前提：如果模型采样出多条推理轨迹，正确答案更可能形成多数，因此多数票可以近似提供有用伪标签。作者指出，这个前提在自由答案空间里较容易成立，因为错误轨迹往往分散到不同字符串上，彼此不容易形成稳定共识；但在四选一这类小答案空间里，不同错误推理很容易“碰撞”到同一个错误选项上，形成虚假的多数共识，并被训练进一步强化。于是模型学到的是更强的答案收缩，而不是更好的推理。\n\n基于这个诊断，作者提出 PROSE，Process Reward Guided Self-Training。它保留 test-time RL 的基本循环：在无标注测试集上采样轨迹、根据奖励更新策略；但把奖励从“最终答案是否与多数票一致”改成“推理过程质量是否可靠”。具体做法是引入一个 medical process reward model，对 reasoning chain 的每一步打分，然后把整条轨迹的 reward 定义为所有 step score 的最小值，而不是均值。这里的设计很关键。用 minimum aggregation 的原因是避免一条轨迹用很多表面流畅、局部正确的步骤去掩盖一个关键错误步骤；只要链路中最薄弱的一步不可靠，整条轨迹就不应得到高奖励。论文还加入了 answer-format guard，防止模型通过格式偏移或非标准输出钻奖励空子。\n\n方法上的一个实用点是，PRM 只在 adaptation 阶段使用，不在最终推理时参与 reranking 或 selection。也就是说，作者希望模型把过程奖励“吸收”进参数，而不是在部署时外挂一个 reward model。这一点和很多 test-time verifier / reranker 路线不同：后者往往推理时成本更高，也更依赖外部模块。PROSE 的目标是学到可迁移的 reasoning behavior，而不是在当前测试集上做一次性筛选。","experiments":"实验首先展示了标准 TTRL 在医疗多选 QA 上的失败模式：sample-level accuracy 只有有限提升，majority-vote accuracy 基本不提升，但输出多样性显著下降。也就是说，模型变得更自洽，却没有更正确。\n\n更关键的是控制变量实验。作者在 AMC 数学题上保持问题、模型、优化器、训练流程不变，只改变答案空间：一份是原始 free-form 作答，一份是匹配的四选一 MCQ。结果显示，free-form 设置下 TTRL 会稳定提升；改成 multiple-choice 后，准确率先小幅上升，随后塌缩。这个实验很有说服力，因为它把“医疗领域更难”这个解释基本排除了，直接把问题指向 answer-space structure。\n\n论文还提出 protection gap 这一诊断量，衡量 pseudo-label reward accuracy 与真实答案准确率之间的差距。作者发现，在自由答案空间中，这个 gap 存在且能提供一定保护，因为错误答案分散；而在小答案空间中，这个 gap 近乎消失，错误轨迹更容易被多数票奖励。这个量化结果支撑了 Lucky Hit 失效的机制解释。\n\n方法结果上，PROSE 相比标准 TTRL 能同时提升 sample-level 与 majority-vote accuracy，并避免输出多样性快速塌缩。作者还强调两个结果：一是 adaptation 后不需要 PRM 参与推理，模型自身就能保留收益；二是这种收益能迁移到未参与 adaptation 的数据集，说明学到的不是某个数据集的答案偏好。另一个重要实验是 reward aggregation 的比较：minimum aggregation 明显优于 mean aggregation，后者更容易被 reward hacking 利用。由于给出的全文片段没有完整结果表，不能可靠填写每个数据集、每个模型的具体百分点，但机制性结论已经相当完整。","reading_guide":"这篇值得认真看问题设定和实验设计，但不必逐段细读所有医学背景。建议先看 Introduction 和 Motivation，重点抓住作者如何把失败从“domain difficulty”剥离到“answer-space structure”。随后直接看 AMC 控制实验和 protection gap 定义，这两部分是整篇最有信息量的地方。\n\n方法部分重点看三点：PRM 是如何训练或构造的；为什么 trajectory reward 取 step score 的最小值而不是均值或加权和；answer-format guard 具体拦截了哪些 reward hacking 行为。实验部分优先看 Figure 1、Figure 2，以及 reward aggregation 的消融。若时间有限，40 分钟左右可以读完关键内容。\n\n值得深挖的点有三个。第一，这个结论是否能推广到非医疗 MCQ、代码选择题、judge-based preference optimization 等其他小标签空间场景。第二，minimum aggregation 虽然抑制 reward hacking，但也可能过度惩罚长链推理中的局部噪声，论文是否分析了 reward variance 和训练稳定性。第三，PRM 只在 adaptation 时使用这一设定很实用，但也意味着 PRM 误差会被写入 policy 参数，长期是否会积累偏差，需要看更多跨分布实验。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies why test-time reinforcement learning fails in small answer spaces. Standard TTRL samples multiple responses for each unlabeled test question, takes the majority-vote answer as a pseudo-label, and updates the model to agree with it. This works reasonably well in free-form math settings, but collapses on medical multiple-choice QA: output diversity shrinks quickly while accuracy barely improves. The core problem is not simply that medical questions are harder. The paper asks a more precise question: why does answer-agreement-based self-training become unreliable when the answer space is small and structured, and can process-level rewards replace answer-level agreement to fix that failure?","importance":"This is a post-training and test-time adaptation paper rather than a pretraining paper. Still, it is relevant to foundation-model research because it exposes a general issue in self-generated supervision: when the model bootstraps from its own outputs, the geometry of the reward space matters. Small answer spaces can turn independent reasoning errors into reinforced pseudo-label errors. That insight extends beyond medical QA. It is relevant to self-consistency methods, pseudo-label self-training, verifier-lite pipelines, and other forms of weak or self-generated supervision. The paper matters because it does more than report a domain-specific failure. It isolates the cause through a controlled experiment and then proposes a concrete alternative: reward the reasoning process rather than final-answer agreement. The contribution is best read as a careful diagnosis of RL signal design under test-time adaptation.","technical_approach":"$2a","experiments":"$2b","reading_guide":"$2c","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看失败诊断而非医学结果；§受控答案空间实验应复现到数学和代码 RLVR。 ","en":"Read for the failure diagnosis, not the medical QA result; the controlled answer-space experiment should be replicated for math and code RLVR."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Kailong Fan","Anqi Pu","Yichen Wu","Wanhua Li","Yicong Li","Hanspeter Pfister","Huafeng Liu","Xiang Li","Quanzheng Li","Ning Guo"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["test-time-RL","process-reward","RLVR","self-training","medical-QA"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16660","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.16660","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.16204","title":"Decoy Direction Optimization: A Post-Hoc Defense Against LLM Abliteration","abstract":"Safety guardrails in open-weight language models can be readily bypassed using Refusal Feature Ablation (RFA), a technique that identifies and projects out a linear refusal direction from the residual stream, often achieving a high attack success rate (ASR) while preserving model capability. Defending against these attacks typically requires computationally expensive safety finetuning for every new checkpoint. We introduce Decoy Direction Optimization (DDO), a fast, post-hoc weight-editing defen","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T18:36:35Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_16204.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是开放权重指令模型容易被 Refusal Feature Ablation 去除拒答能力的问题，并尝试用无需重新微调的权重编辑来提高对这类白盒攻击的鲁棒性。","importance":"它不是预训练方法，但对理解 post-train 后的安全表示几何有参考价值。拒答能力往往集中在低维 residual-stream 方向上，这对开放模型发布、对齐稳定性和表示可编辑性都有影响。DDO coordinate，写端向与真实拒答方向近似正交的高幅度 decoy directions 写入信号。RFA 通常用 harmful 与 safe activation 的 difference-in-means 来估计拒答方向；DDO 让这个 contrast 被 decoy 信号主导，使攻击者投影掉的是诱饵方向，而不是真正的拒答子空间。理论部分给出 subspace overlap bound，并在正交 decoy 情况下说明 decoy 强度和 effective decoy rank 如何限制攻击方向与真实拒答子空间的重叠。它还给出一个谱分配原则：固定 decoy 能量时，把能量较均匀地分到 k 个方向，有助于提高第 k 个奇异值，从而增强对 rank-k contrastive ablation 的抵抗。","experiments":"实验使用三层 standard RFA 下达到相近或更好的效果，单配置成本低 30 到 450 倍。面对 adaptive multi-phase RFA，Llama-3-8B-Instruct 的 worst-case ASR 为 65%，接近最强训练式 baseline 的 58%，同时 MT-Bench 不低于 5.82。面对 Heretic，ASR 从 88.7% 降到 18%，设置为 200 本身，而是它暴露出的表示事实：一个 post-trained safety behavior 可以被低维方向估计和权重级编辑显著影响。理论证明可快速浏览，实验表格中 standard RFA、multi-phase RFA、Heretic 三类攻击的差异更值得看。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a white-box safety failure mode in open-weight instruction models: refusal behavior can often be removed by Refusal Feature Ablation, which estimates a linear refusal direction in the residual stream and projects it out while preserving much of the model’s general capability.","importance":"This is not a pretraining method, but it is relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface and to representation geometry. It shows that aligned refusal behavior can depend heavily on low-dimensional features, and that a released checkpoint can be modified without full finetuning to either remove or harden that behavior. For foundation-model researchers, the useful signal is about how fragile post-trained safety circuits can be when they are linearly identifiable.","technical_approach":"Decoy Direction Optimization is a post-hoc weight-editing defense. It repurposes a small set of low-impact MLP neurons into a gated read-write mechanism. On harmful prompts, these neurons read the refusal coordinate and write large signals into directions that are approximately orthogonal to the true refusal direction. Since standard RFA estimates refusal with contrastive difference-in-means between harmful and safe activations, the injected decoy dominates the contrast and causes the attacker to ablate the decoy rather than the causal refusal subspace. The paper provides subspace overlap bounds and specializes them to orthogonal decoys. It also introduces effective decoy rank as a diagnostic for how many adaptive re-estimation phases the defense can absorb, and gives a spectral allocation principle: under a fixed energy budget, spreading decoy energy across k directions improves the k-th singular value and helps against rank-k contrastive ablation.","experiments":"The evaluation uses a three-level training-free attack ladder: standard RFA, adaptive multi-phase RFA, and Heretic. Across six model families, DDO reduces standard-RFA attack success rate to below 10%, with each optimization run taking about 2 minutes on a single A100. On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, standard RFA ASR drops from 85% to 1.8%. Compared with trained defenses, DDO matches or exceeds standard-RFA robustness at 30 to 450 times lower per-configuration cost. Under adaptive multi-phase RFA on Llama-3-8B-Instruct, the worst-case ASR is 65%, compared with 58% for the strongest trained baseline, while maintaining coherent generation with MT-Bench at least 5.82. Against Heretic, ASR is reduced from 88.7% to 18% at 200 trials. The defense is compiled into the weights and adds no inference-time overhead.","reading_guide":"Skim the Introduction, method section, and attack evaluation sections; 30 to 45 minutes should be enough. Focus on how the gated decoy is implemented and how effective decoy rank explains degradation under adaptive re-estimation. For pretraining-oriented readers, the main value is not the safety defense recipe itself, but the evidence that post-trained refusal can be strongly shaped by low-dimensional, linearly estimated directions. The theory can be read lightly; the standard RFA, multi-phase RFA, and Heretic comparisons are the most informative parts.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"从机制可解释性角度提出了一种巧妙的防御策略，通过注入诱饵信号对抗特征消融，对理解和增强模型安全性有启发。","en":"Proposes an ingenious defense strategy from a mechanistic interpretability perspective, injecting decoy signals to counter feature ablation, inspiring for understanding and enhancing model safety."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Aashiq Muhamed","Mona T. Diab","Virginia Smith"],"tags":["safety guardrails","refusal feature ablation","mechanistic interpretability","weight editing","decoy signal"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16204","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.16204","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.16648","title":"GrowMTP: Can RL Grow Its Own Draft Head?","abstract":"Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training drives the frontier capabilities of large language models, with its wall-clock dominated by autoregressive rollout generation. Speculative decoding is an established remedy for this bottleneck, but existing draft heads must be pretrained or warmed up before RL, introducing substantial training cost outside the RL run to be accelerated. We observe that RL training itself provides both conditions required for online draft-head training: its rollout distrib","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-15T05:09:12Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_16648.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很现实的问题：RL 后训练里最贵的部分往往不是策略更新，而是 rollout 的自回归生成；而 speculative decoding 虽然能加速生成，但现有 draft head 通常需要在 RL 之前离线预训练或 warmup，导致“为了加速 RL，先额外做一轮不便宜的训练”。论文要解决的核心矛盾是：能不能不依赖预先存在的 MTP/draft head，而是在 RL 过程中直接把 draft head 长出来，并且尽快产生净加速。作者的判断依据有两个：RL rollout 的分布比通用预训练分布窄得多，draft head 不需要学会通用 drafting；同时 speculative verification 本身会持续产生与当前 rollout 分布对齐的监督信号，这些信号过去基本被浪费了。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是在改 pretrain objective，但它很有价值，因为它把 pretrain 才有的“原生 MTP 头”能力，部分迁移成了 post-train 阶段可在线生长的模块。这个接口意义不小：第一，它说明某些 traditionally 需要在预训练阶段铺好的能力，并不一定都要在 pretrain 里一次性做完，至少在分布更窄的 RL 阶段，可以用运行中产生的监督补出来。第二，它给 MTP/head 训练提供了一个新的视角：draft head 的有效性高度依赖目标分布，通用预训练得到的 head 未必是 RL rollout 上最合适的 head。第三，它对 RL 训练系统设计有直接启发——如果 rollout verification 已经在算，就应该尽量把这些信号回收成训练收益，而不是只把它当作解码时的控制逻辑。贡献量级上，这更像一个很实用的系统-算法接口创新，而不是改变基座模型范式的工作。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 GrowMTP：在 RL loop 内，利用 speculative decoding 的 verification 信号在线训练一个多 token 预测头。难点不在“有监督信号”，而在“什么监督才是有效监督”。作者抓了三个关键约束。第一，训练时的条件状态必须和 rollout 时一致。因为第 k 个 draft token 的预测依赖前面 draft token，而不是依赖最终被 target 接受后的真实前缀；如果直接拿执行后的前缀做监督，会出现 train-test mismatch。GrowMTP 因此显式记录 draft trajectory，并重建 draft-conditioned hidden states，在产生该 draft 的状态上施加监督。第二，不同 drafting depth 的学习是耦合的。更深层 token 只有在前面 token 被接受时才有价值，所以不能把每个 depth 当成独立分类任务来平均训练。为此作者设计了 depth-coupled acceptance, DCA loss，用 acceptance chain surrogate 去近似“整条可接受链”的收益，并对链值做对数变换，让较小 chain value 的样本也能得到更强相对梯度，避免训练只偏向浅层高频位置。第三，first rejection 之后的更深位置其实对应的是不会被执行的 suffix；这些位置虽然也有 verification 输出，但继续训练会把 head 往无效条件分布上推。GrowMTP 因此在首次拒绝后做 gating，截断后续 loss。另一个重要设计是把 draft-head 更新与 policy backbone 解耦，梯度不回传到主干，避免 draft 学习干扰 RL policy learning。这样做的取舍很明确：牺牲了联合优化的可能收益，换来训练稳定性和模块化。论文还强调，不需要 depth curriculum，也不需要先有一个弱 head；因为第一个 draft 位置总有有效监督，所以可以从随机初始化直接开始长。","experiments":"最关键的实验是从零开始训练 head。作者在没有预训练 MTP 头的 Qwen3-4B 上，直接挂一个随机初始化的 K=5 draft head，进行 500 个 math RL steps。结果是 acceptance length 从接近自回归下限逐步长到 2.91，rollout 加速达到 2.13×，端到端 step speedup 达到 1.60×。更重要的是，这个数字已经把在线 head 训练成本算进去了；在 500 steps 总预算下，相比“先离线训练一个达到相近 acceptance 的 head 再做 RL”，总 GPU-hours 还能下降 9.4%。这说明它不是把成本从 A 挪到 B，而是在有限 RL run 中确实有净收益。第二类实验是对已有 head 的继续在线适配。在 Qwen3.5-4B-Base 上，在线更新相对 frozen head 还能带来 1.36× rollout speedup，说明预训练 head 不是 rollout 分布上的最优点。在 MiMo-7B-SFT 上，作者把原本 K=1 的 head 扩展到 K=5，acceptance 从 1.65 提升到 4.0，rollout speedup 达到 1.93×。还有一个有意思的结果是 domain specificity：在 Math 和 Code rollout 上分别训练出的 head，在各自域内 acceptance 更高，支持“draft head 应该贴近 rollout 分布”这个前提。摘要里没有给出更细的 acceptance-by-depth、训练曲线和各 loss 组件的完整数字，但从引言信息看，论文至少做了从零训练、已有 head 继续训练、跨 domain 适配这三类验证，实验设计是完整的。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着系统问题去读。先看 Introduction，把作者提出的矛盾想清楚：为什么 speculative decoding 在 serving 里成熟，在 RL 里却还依赖离线 head。然后重点看方法部分里关于 draft-conditioned state reconstruction、DCA loss、以及 first-rejection gating 的定义。这三处决定了它不是简单地“把 verification logits 拿来蒸馏”。如果时间有限，优先看方法图、loss 公式和训练流程伪代码，再看从随机 head 长出来的主实验曲线，关注 acceptance length 随 RL step 的增长速度，以及何时开始出现净加速。消融部分值得看三点：一是如果不用 draft-conditioned reconstruction，会不会出现明显的 acceptance 损失；二是如果把各 depth 独立训练，是否会导致深层 token 学不好；三是如果不在 first rejection 后截断，是否会学到错误 suffix。阅读时可以顺手思考两个延伸问题：其一，这种在线长 head 的思路能否推广到更强的 native MTP 或 EAGLE 类结构，而不只是轻量 head；其二，如果允许 backbone 轻微参与更新，是否能进一步提升 acceptance，还是会破坏 RL 稳定性。预计泛读 20-30 分钟能抓住主线，细读方法和实验大约 1 小时。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a practical bottleneck in RL post-training: most wall-clock time is spent on autoregressive rollout generation rather than on the RL update itself. Speculative decoding is a known remedy, but existing draft heads usually need to be pretrained or warmed up before RL starts, which adds a separate training bill before any acceleration is realized. The core question is whether RL can grow its own draft head online, from scratch if needed, and obtain net speedup early enough to matter. The paper argues yes, because RL rollouts are much narrower in distribution than broad pretraining data, and speculative verification already produces aligned supervision signals at every step.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it matters for foundation-model training because it weakens the assumption that useful MTP-style drafting capability must be installed during pretraining. If r(b)=1/b, every tree delivers exactly one finding regardless of task size or tree shape. If r(b)=C b^{-δ}, a depth-k tree over N findings yields C^k N^{1-δ}.
